icc2_shell> # Block/Top: i2c_master_top
icc2_shell> # Technology: saed90nm
icc2_shell> # EDA Tool: Synopsys (ICC2)
icc2_shell> # Script Name : cts.tcl
icc2_shell> # Usage : icc2_shell -f script/cts.tcl -output_log log/cts.log
icc2_shell> # Prerequisites: designlib created
icc2_shell> # Creation Date: 2026-09-19
icc2_shell> # Educational : ITI_summer_camp_2026_R3
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> # --------------- Variable setup ------------- #
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> set design "i2c"
i2c
icc2_shell> set std_worst "saed90nm_max.db"
saed90nm_max.db
icc2_shell> set target_library $std_worst
saed90nm_max.db
icc2_shell> set corner "worst"
worst
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> set project_dir "/mnt/hgfs/Shared_files/${design}"
/mnt/hgfs/Shared_files/i2c
icc2_shell> set Lib_path    "/home/ICer/Downloads/Lib"
/home/ICer/Downloads/Lib
icc2_shell> set dlib_path   "${project_dir}/pnr/2_design_lib/library"
/mnt/hgfs/Shared_files/i2c/pnr/2_design_lib/library
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> set prev_stage      "placement"
placement
icc2_shell> set current_stage   "cts"
cts
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> # --------------- Files Handling ------------- #
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> sh rm    -r ../results/reports
Error: rm: cannot remove '../results/reports': No such file or directory
        Use error_info for more info. (CMD-013)
icc2_shell> sh rm    -r ../results/outputs
Error: rm: cannot remove '../results/outputs': No such file or directory
        Use error_info for more info. (CMD-013)
icc2_shell> sh mkdir -p ../results/reports
icc2_shell> sh mkdir -p ../results/outputs
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> # --------------- dlib setup ------------- #
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> open_block  ${dlib_path}/${design}.dlib:${design}_${prev_stage}.design
Information: User units loaded from library 'saed90nm_max' (LNK-040)
Opening block 'i2c.dlib:i2c_placement.design' in edit mode
Information: loading PG routing via master rules, patterns, strategies and strategy via rules.
{i2c.dlib:i2c_placement.design}
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> # copying the (clean) block from the previous stage (dlib) to just preserve it and work on the new one which is the present stage (floorplan)
icc2_shell> #to make sure if any errors happened through the current stage, it will be easy to restore the previous stage
icc2_shell> copy_block -from_block ${design}.dlib:${design}_${prev_stage}.design -to_block ${design}_${current_stage}
{i2c.dlib:i2c_cts.design}
icc2_shell> 
icc2_shell> current_block ${design}_${current_stage}.design
{i2c.dlib:i2c_cts.design}
icc2_shell> start_gui
Begin building search trees for block i2c.dlib:i2c_cts.design
Done building search trees for block i2c.dlib:i2c_cts.design (time 0s)
icc2_shell> link_block
Using libraries: i2c.dlib saed90nm_max saed90_ndm_physical_only
Warning: In library i2c.dlib, no block views exist for block i2c_master_top. (LNK-064)
Visiting block i2c.dlib:i2c_cts.design
Design 'i2c_master_top' was successfully linked.
1
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> # --------------- Tool setup ------------- #
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> set_host_option -max_cores 12;            # multi threading is used to get the results faster (must be a high-end laptop)
1
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> # --------------- Pre-CTS Checks ------------- #
icc2_shell> #######################################################################
icc2_shell> report_qor -summary  > ../results/reports/qor_pre_cts.rpt
icc2_shell> # Clock sources
icc2_shell> report_clocks  > ../results/reports/clocks.rpt
icc2_shell> # setting the routing resources for CTS to be only on the layers from M3 to M8 (using a stack of vias to route the clock pin "from M1" on M3)
icc2_shell> set_ignored_layers -max_routing_layer M8 -min_routing_layer M3
Information: The net parasitics of block i2c_master_top are cleared. (TIM-123)
1
icc2_shell> report_clocks

    G - Generated  clock
